## Summary

- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 22059 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness

- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
